Fixed: random full-64-bit pairs (seed 0x13A6E13), matching the real hash-finalization input model. (3) Smoke (random domain, 2^16 pairs): BOYAR19 6st z=141.0 (d=0x3), ULBC_s1 6st z=168.9 (d=0x1) -- real differential signal, above the z<25 bar; 4st still diffusion-incomplete (constbits=1447). **Full 21-try triage LAUNCHED 21:32Z** (nice-19 via agentwork-launch.sh, backoff CLEAR 11.12; log adder_lat/triage_wave13.log, ~1h). **Last updated:** 2026-10-10 17:50 CEST (Track 8, inc 6): **Wave-31 Axis B (majr-replacement) DIAGNOSED DEAD — structural:** `x := maj(ra,rb,rc)^rd` has a DETERMINISTIC 1-bit differential trail (input p -> output p+d, prob 1) when d not in {a,b,c}: at out-bit p+d the maj inputs (p+d-a,p+d-b,p+d-c) never include p, so Dmaj=0 and Dout=1 always -> DIFFz=256. Forcing d=a (overlap) removes determinism BUT exposes weak diffusion: maj(ra,rb,rc)^ra = (ra&~rb&~rc)^(rb&rc), and flipping ra flips output only iff rb=rc=0 (P=0.25/stage) -> strong bias (P(diff)=0.044, z=90.5). **Principle: replacement form needs a BIJECTIVE nonlinearity (S-box); non-bijective N loses deltas.** Axis B killed, not launched as search. Axis A (two-state) also showed z=90.5 bias (out-bit 0) — DEPRIORITIZED pending redesign. Live axis: **C (S-box layer + fused diffusion)** — fz31_s1/s2/s3 built, bit-exact 200/200, S0 pending load. | 5-stage DIFF wall (ownerless lead) | **THEOREM (structural)** | 2026-10-10 | **Track 3 root-cause analysis COMPLETE.** 5 stages of 2-input AND/OR-with-rotation CANNOT achieve S2 differential cleanliness for ANY rotation schedule. **Mechanism (AND masking lemma, exact + verified):** for stage F(x)=x^(R_a(x)&R_b(x))^R_c(x) with input difference d, E[(-1)^{(dA)_j}] = 1 iff d_{j-a}=d_{j-b}=0 (AND transparent), else 0 (AND randomizes). Each stage only randomizes "touched" output bits. **Proof:** last-stage pairs {j-a_5,j-b_5} are cyclic distance-d pairs (d=b_5-a_5); S2 requires P[hit]>99.1% for all pairs, but min_d max_k P[miss at distance d] = 0.50 for the 4-stage difference distribution (N=2^16 MC) -- 55x above the 0.009 threshold. NO (a_5,b_5) can fix it. **Falsifications:** linear-skeleton diffusion hypothesis FALSIFIED (and5xa skeleton weight 32 > and6a's 28); 5-stage prefixes of GOOD and6a schedule FAIL (first5 z=16.8, last5 z=38.3) -- structural, not schedule. Same quiet condition holds for chi, so wall covers AND/chi/hetero/3-term-linear. **Scope:** 2-input AND/OR-with-rotation cascades. **Re-test trigger:** NEW nonlinear operation (not 2-input AND/OR) with better per-stage masking. **Implication:** 6-stage minimum is structural; 20.1u = 6x3.35u is the floor for this operation family. Full analysis: `adder_lat/rootcause/ROOTCAUSE.md`. |

FIXED as repo commit `8a51606c` (group now includes matrix.combo.idx; verified: 14 shards ran with 0 cancellations). Escape-protocol addendum: quote the check-run annotation when diagnosing "cancellations" — the annotation names the exact mechanism.** Process patch (escape protocol): never posit an external cancelling actor without run-timeline evidence (who cancelled, which run, timestamps); status reports must quote the schedule-time vs run-time distinction. Batch rule ≤17 specs/push is the standing fix. | mix-search-status-30m |

| D9 analytic result | — | **D9 COMPLETE 2026-10-10 (Track 4, cost-optimization)**: hypothesis FALSIFIED — the 27.2u floor has no significant fat. (1) Depth-2 S-box (Midori Sb0) saves 0.1-0.4u, not ~2u: the S-box is not on the critical path (LIN+chi dominate); D8's claim holds — 2-XOR LIN on all rounds is what breaks the invariant, and the Midori swap does NOT reintroduce it (D=18-32, multi-key stable, probes in-band). (2) Rotation-pair choice is synthesis noise (27.1-27.4u). (3) wideA chi triples cost +0.5u. (4) CSCS pattern cost-neutral; chi-first does NOT allow LIN relaxation (DIFF-probe-dead). (5) keccak+Midori (27.5u) worse than keccak+rasC2_0 — savings do not stack. **Cost floor ≈ 27.1u** (dd12_scsc_midori_12_a, 6249 cells). Screen tooling: d9_screen.py (generalized D-screen + DIFF probe; caught+fixed a LIN-application bug via the D8 positive control before use — every generalized screen must carry a committed control). | D8 analytic result | — | **D8 COMPLETE 2026-10-10 (Track 4, analytic pivot)**: (1) **Invariant characterized**: the S1 LIN/DIFF "invariant subspace" is a DIFFUSION defect — on 64-consecutive inputs, nolin outputs have low algebraic degree (18 constant bits, rank ~25); the varying support grows too slowly for S-box/chi to build degree. Single-rot[13] fails differently (D=1923, rank ~61): 1-XOR rotation layers are insufficient EVEN WHEN BIOJECTIVE (rot13+passthrough: D=1659) — the rotation structure itself is inadequate, not just the 2-to-1-ness. (2) **Cheapest provably-working LIN**: any bijective 2-XOR rotation pair on ALL rounds (lin513/pair13-27/pair1-2/pair7-19: D≈20 multi-key clean). Proven minimal by exhaustive analytic screen: all 1-XOR fail, all partial placements fail (sbox-only D=281, chi-only dead, drop-r1/r2 dead); last LIN load-bearing for DIFF (low-8-bit marginal) though rank-invariant. (3) **Triple co-design**: close-AND-tap structure load-bearing; CHI_A near-optimal; CHI_B wide catastrophic (D=3720); wideA marginally better DIFF probe. (4) **3-round chi-first (CCS/CSC/CSS)**: LIN-clean (D=20-33) but DIFF-dead — scoped negative. (5) **Scoped verdict**: mixed SPN family cannot beat al_and6a 20.1u — 3r DIFF-dead at ~20.5u, 4r S1-clean at 27.2u. | **D9: pivot Track 4** — (a) cost-optimize the 4r S1-clean construction (S-box depth, ~24u target), or (b) stand down frontier chase (T8 has sub-20u leads: tchi3a 18.4u) | Track 4 |

**Durable finding (carry-save/majority):** Redundant-state carry-save avoids carry propagation but the XOR4 (4.4u isolated) and MAJ3+XOR overhead make it 4.0-4.4u/stage -- 30% slower than AND-cascade (3.35u/stage). Majority is balanced (better LIN potential) but costs 1u/stage more than AND. The new axis is S0-dominated; S1 quality unknown.

**Durable findings (3-term-linear family):**
1. 4-stage STRUCTURALLY DEAD (fz16_a with fz15_c's own rotations still dies) -- family needs >=5 stages
2. 5-stage needs 4 INDEPENDENT rotations for diffusion (fz15_c clean; any reuse kills DIFF)
3. 4.5u/stage cost is fundamental to the 4-rotation structure -- family floor ~22.6u
4. Balanced XOR tree doesn't help; small rotations slower; hetero with chi dilutes diffusion
5. fz15_c (22.6u, S1+S2 clean) remains the ONLY clean 3-term-linear design

**Frontier:** al_and6a 20.1u (unchanged). fz15_c is S1+S2 clean but dominated on speed.
